See https://reactjs.org/link/uselayouteffect-ssr for common fixes.

Se falhar, o Gradle aborta.\n// Builds debug não são afectados.\ndef frontendDir = rootProject.projectDir.parentFile\ndef validarAmbienteProducao = tasks.register("validarAmbienteProducao", Exec) {\n    group = "verification"\n    description = "Recusa o build release se o bundle web não tiver o env de produção (mainnet)."\n    workingDir = frontendDir\n    commandLine "node", "scripts/validar-dist-rede.mjs", file("src/main/assets/public").absolutePath\n    outputs.upToDateWhen { false }\n}\ntasks.configureEach { t ->\n    if (t.name == "preReleaseBuild") {\n        t.dependsOn validarAmbienteProducao\n    }\n}\n\ntry {\n    def servicesJSON = file(\'google-services.json\')\n    if (servicesJSON.text) {\n        apply plugin: \'com.google.gms.google-services\'\n    }\n} catch(Exception e) {\n    logger.info("google-services.json not found, google-services plugin not applied. Push Notifications won\'t work")\n}\n',
    expected: /^\s*versionCode 15\s*$/m,
    operator: 'match',
    diff: 'simple'
  }
